          🛠️ 𝗠𝗼𝗱-𝗢𝗻𝗹𝘆 𝗧𝗿𝗶𝗴𝗴𝗲𝗿𝘀
          These formats are public reference. Their effects activate only when the sender has moderator/admin status.
          :Blank:
          𝗧𝗮𝗿𝗴𝗲𝘁𝗲𝗱 𝗺𝗼𝗱𝗲𝗿𝗮𝘁𝗶𝗼𝗻
          ➤ @yeet \[username\] (nickname) full-ID
          Fires immediately on the matching user’s browser: the curse is stored and they are ejected. A later @yeet still works after @bail.
          ➤ @3d \[username\] (nickname) full-ID
          Covers the matching user’s page with the 3D image at the highest UI layer. A small inset top-right ✕ looks like dismiss, but clicking it enters browser fullscreen. The sticky image remains until @bail.
          ➤ @warn \[username\] (nickname) full-ID -- reason \[span:3x\]
          Creates or replaces that user’s current warning. Omit span for once; 3x means three displays. Time spans support h, d, w, m, or y, such as \[span:5d\].
          ➤ @bail \[username\] (nickname) full-ID
          Moderator mercy and troubleshooting override. Removes that user’s pending or stored yeet/3D/age/word curses and warnings, then records both adult-room rule notices as accepted on that browser.
          @bail is not immunity from moderators. A later @warn, @yeet, or @3d applies normally and replaces the relevant state; use @bail again to free them again.
          @bail does not bypass the adult nickname age rule. A clear adult age is still required, and a permanent minor-age ban is not cleared by @bail.
          :Blank:
          For @yeet, @3d, @warn, and @bail, username, nickname, and full-ID are each optional independent identifiers. Example: \[rudbo\] (36M | perchance.org/furpost#hETpnf1VxgS1) 3FML-60b69ae55a339fad58d6
          The -- delimiter belongs to @warn and separates the target from its reason. \[span:...\] also belongs only to @warn.
          The moderator who posts a targeted command receives a local confirmation showing whether it was accepted.
          :Blank:
          𝗡𝗼𝘁𝗶𝗰𝗲𝘀 𝗮𝗻𝗱 𝗿𝗲𝗳𝗿𝗲𝘀𝗵
          ➤ @all message — Send a general announcement popup.
          ➤ @pin message — Keep an important notice pinned until dismissed.
          ➤ @news optional details — Announce a new feature and offer manual refresh.
          ➤ @update optional details — Announce a site update and recommend refresh.
          ➤ @refresh optional message — Start the normal refresh flow; respects each user’s auto-refresh setting.
          ➤ @hardrefresh optional reason — Force an uncancellable reload countdown. The trigger uses @, not /. Use only for urgent broken state, raids, or required recovery.
          ➤ @ban optional note — Announce that a ban was completed and ask users to refresh. It does not select or ban the target by itself.
          :Blank:
          𝗢𝘁𝗵𝗲𝗿 𝗺𝗼𝗱 𝘁𝗿𝗶𝗴𝗴𝗲𝗿𝘀
          ➤ ban rain — Admin-authored phrase that starts the ban-rain effect.
          ➤ Message ending in “last warning” — Shows the global last-warning notice.
          ➤ :WARNING_column1::WARNING_column2::WARNING_column3::WARNING_column4::WARNING_column5: markers — Global warning notice; prefer @warn for targeted warnings.
          ➤ /shutup — Its marker triggers the global ignore/refresh notice only when posted by a moderator.
          $output = ["\n"+this.joinItems("\n")]

Lower values allow more creative freedom and can produce richer, more complex results but may become messy; higher values follow the prompt more strictly and give clearer outputs, though they may look more cartoonish. Use (guidanceScale:::X) to override it, where X ranges from 1–30. Lower values allow more creative freedom and can produce richer, more complex results but may become messy; higher values follow the prompt more strictly and give clearer outputs, though they may look more cartoonish. If multiple guidance scales appear, only the last one will be applied.

// commentChannels 
  commentChannels
    general
      label = General
      commentPlaceholderText = Type a friendly, 𝗦𝗙𝗪 comment and read the rules ⓘ avoid risky links
      allowBots = false // disallows 'speak via character' for this channel, default is allowed except for general
      bannedWords = [jail.PG13BannedWords] // PG ban words + regular ban words
      beforeSubmit(data) =>
        if (!allowPg13ProfileSubmit("chat")) return null
      
    roleplay
      label = Roleplay
      commentPlaceholderText = Start a 𝗦𝗙𝗪 roleplay or type your character's action...
      submitButtonText = send roleplay
      bannedWords = [jail.PG13BannedWords] // PG ban words + regular ban words
      beforeSubmit(data) =>
        if (!allowPg13ProfileSubmit("chat")) return null
      onComment(comment) =>
        baseOnComment(comment, "", "chat");
        
        const u = comment.user;
        const hasPfp = u && (u.avatar || u.avatarUrl);
        const isTrueAnon = !u || (!u.name && !u.nickname && !u.nick && !hasPfp);
        const hasMessage = comment.message && comment.message.trim();

        if (window?.perchNotifyToggle === false) return;
        
        // Broadcast to mods if an anon is trolling the RP chat
        if (isTrueAnon && hasMessage) {
          const vid = (u && u.visualId) ? String(u.visualId).toUpperCase() : "ANON";
          window._alertedMods = window._alertedMods || new Set();
          
          if (!window._alertedMods.has(vid + "_anon_rp")) {
            window._alertedMods.add(vid + "_anon_rp");
            notifyPinned("Heads up! Anonymous user [" + vid + "] is attempting to roleplay without a name.");
          }
        }

  galleryOptions
    gallery = true
    sort = recent
    adaptiveHeight = true // <-- expand gallery height so it never has its own scrollbar
    style = background-color: var(--background-color)
    contentFilter = G
    defaultGalleryNames

    bannedUsers = [jail.bannedGalleryList] // Now Moved to external link; click the settings button at the top of the gallery and type "admin" to toggle admin mode on, then double-click on an image to get the user ID of the creator.
    bannedPromptPhrases = [jail.bannedPromptPhrases]
    bannedNegativePromptPhrases = [jail.bannedNegativePromptPhrases]
          
    customButton
      emoji = ➕︎
      onClick(data) =>
        window._currentImageData = data
        showModal(`
          <div style="padding: 10px; max-width: 90vw; font-family: sans-serif;">
            <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; font-size: 0.9rem; min-width: 200px;">
      
              <button onclick="showImageFullscreen('${data.imageUrl}')" style="
                display: flex;
                justify-content: flex-start;
                align-items: center;
                gap: 0.5em;
                padding: 6px 10px;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                background: var(--box-color);
                color: var(--text-color);
                cursor: pointer;
                text-align: left;
              " onmouseover="this.style.opacity='0.85'" onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
                <i class='fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass-plus fa-fw'></i> zoom image
              </button>
      
              <button onclick="saveToPrivateGallery()" style="
                display: flex;
                justify-content: flex-start;
                align-items: center;
                gap: 0.5em;
                padding: 6px 10px;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                background: var(--box-color);
                color: var(--text-color);
                cursor: pointer;
                text-align: left;
              " onmouseover="this.style.opacity='0.85'" onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-notes-medical fa-fw"></i> save to concept board
              </button>
      
              <button onclick="
                (() => {
                  const btn = this;
                  fetch('${data.imageUrl}')
                    .then(r => r.blob())
                    .then(b => {
                      const a = document.createElement('a');
                      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(b);
                      a.download = 'image';
                      a.click();
                      URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
                      const original = btn.innerHTML;
                      btn.innerHTML = '<i class=\\'fa-solid fa-check fa-fw\\'></i> saving...';
                      setTimeout(() => btn.innerHTML = original, 2000);

isNsfwGatedGalleryImage(data) =>
  // An image is age-gated for gallery comments when its prompt carries the
  // U1F51E (🔞 NSFW) label token, OR when the platform gallery's real
  // moderation metadata marks it as NSFW (data-is-nsfw="true").
  if (!data) return false
  if (data.isNsfw === true || data.isNsfw === "true") return true
  if (String(data.prompt || "").includes("U1F51E")) return true
  return false

$output(txt) =>
  // Clean expired hard-refresh command IDs once per page load.
  // Per-comment keys prevent replayed @hardrefresh commands from triggering again during the 24-hour window.
  if (!window._hardRefreshDedupCleanupDone) {
    window._hardRefreshDedupCleanupDone = true
    const now = Date.now()
    Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(key => {
      if (!key.startsWith("hr_cmd_")) return
      const savedAt = Number(localStorage.getItem(key) || 0)
      if (!Number.isFinite(savedAt) || now - savedAt > 86400000) {
        localStorage.removeItem(key)
      }
    })
    localStorage.removeItem("lastHardRefreshCommandId")
  }

  // Initialize the command list once.
  // 1. (FIX) Always define the command list data if it doesn't exist.
  // We use a separate check, so it runs even if the other plugin sets the UUID.
  if (!window.commandLists) {
    // Helper function for case-insensitive alphabetical sorting
    const alphaSort = (a, b) => a.toLowerCase().localeCompare(b.toLowerCase());
    const moderatorMentions = [
      '3d', 'all', 'bail', 'ban', 'hardrefresh', 'news', 'pin', 'refresh', 'update', 'warn', 'yeet'

          item.textContent = cmd;
          item.style.cssText = `
            padding: 4px 8px;
            border-radius: 4px;
          `;
 
          item.onmouseenter = () => item.style.backgroundColor = 'light-dark(#f0f0f0, #3f4147)';
          item.onmouseleave = () => item.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
          resultsList.appendChild(item);
        });
      }
    }
  } else {
    if (popup) popup.remove();
  }

// Imported from furry-banlist — full-ID, nickname, username, combo yeet & warn
yeetChatID = [jail.yeetChatID]
yeetNickNames = [jail.yeetNickNames]
yeetUsernames = [jail.yeetUsernames]
yeetNameCombos = [jail.yeetNameCombos]
warnChatNames = [jail.warnChatNames]
warnChatWords = [jail.warnChatWords]

normalizeChatFullId(value) =>
  const match = String(value || "").trim().match(/^([a-z0-9]{4})-([a-z0-9]+)$/i)
  return match ? `${match[1].toUpperCase()}-${match[2].toLowerCase()}` : ""

getYeetChatIdSet() =>
  if (window._yeetChatIdCache !== undefined) {
    return window._yeetChatIdCache
  }

  const items = yeetChatID?.selectAll
  if (!items) return new Set()

  const ids = new Set()
  for (const item of items) {
    const value = String(item.evaluateItem ?? item.getName ?? item ?? "").trim()
    if (!value || value.startsWith("//")) continue

    const id = normalizeChatFullId(value)
    if (id) ids.add(id)
  }

  window._yeetChatIdCache = ids
  return ids

isUserBannedByFullId(passportId) =>
  const id = normalizeChatFullId(passportId)
  return !!id && getYeetChatIdSet().has(id)

getChatYeetWords() =>
  if (window._chatYeetWordsCache !== undefined) {
    return window._chatYeetWordsCache
  }

  const items = jail.chatYeetWords?.selectAll
  if (!items) return []

  const compiled = items.map(item => {
    const value = item.getName ?? item

    if (value instanceof RegExp) {
      return value
    }

    if (typeof value === "string") {
      const match = value.match(/^\/([\s\S]*)\/([a-z]*)$/i)
      if (!match) return null

      try {
        return new RegExp(match[1], match[2])
      } catch {
        return null
      }
    }

    return null
  }).filter(Boolean)

  window._chatYeetWordsCache = compiled
  return compiled

getYeetNameRegexes(listValue, cacheKey) =>
  if (window[cacheKey] !== undefined) {
    return window[cacheKey]
  }

  const items = listValue?.selectAll
  if (!items) return []

  const regexes = []
  for (const item of items) {
    const value = String(item.evaluateItem || "").trim()
    if (!value || value.startsWith("//")) continue

    const match = value.match(/^\/([\s\S]*)\/([a-z]*)$/i)
    if (!match) continue

    try {
      regexes.push(new RegExp(match[1], match[2] || "i"))
    } catch {}
  }

  window[cacheKey] = regexes
  return regexes

matchesYeetNameList(value, listValue, cacheKey) =>
  if (!value) return false

  const regexes = getYeetNameRegexes(listValue, cacheKey)
  for (const rx of regexes) {
    if (rx.global || rx.sticky) rx.lastIndex = 0
    if (rx.test(value)) return true
  }

  return false

getYeetNameComboRegexes(listValue, cacheKey) =>
  if (window[cacheKey] !== undefined) {
    return window[cacheKey]
  }

  const items = listValue?.selectAll
  if (!items) return []

  const combos = []
  for (const item of items) {
    const value = String(item.evaluateItem || "").trim()
    if (!value || value.startsWith("//")) continue

    // Banlist format follows chat identity order: /username regex/flags -- /nickname regex/flags
    const match = value.match(/^\/((?:\\.|[^/])*)\/([a-z]*)\s*--\s*\/((?:\\.|[^/])*)\/([a-z]*)$/i)
    if (!match) continue

    try {
      combos.push({
        username: new RegExp(match[1], match[2] || "i"),
        nickname: new RegExp(match[3], match[4] || "i")
      })
    } catch {}
  }

  window[cacheKey] = combos
  return combos

matchesYeetNameCombos(username, nickname) =>
  const combos = getYeetNameComboRegexes(yeetNameCombos, "_yeetNameCombosCache")

  for (const combo of combos) {
    if (combo.username.global || combo.username.sticky) combo.username.lastIndex = 0
    if (combo.nickname.global || combo.nickname.sticky) combo.nickname.lastIndex = 0

    // AND inside one combo rule. Different combo lines are OR alternatives.
    if (combo.username.test(username) && combo.nickname.test(nickname)) return true
  }

  return false

isUserBannedByNicknameOrUsername(nickname, username) =>
  const nick = String(nickname || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
  const user = String(username || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
  if (!nick && !user) return null

  // Special nickname+username combinations are data-driven by furry-banlist.
  if (matchesYeetNameCombos(user, nick)) return true

  // Ordinary lists remain independent OR matches:
  // nickname hit OR username hit => yeet.
  if (matchesYeetNameList(nick, yeetNickNames, "_yeetNickNamesCache")) return true
  if (matchesYeetNameList(user, yeetUsernames, "_yeetUsernamesCache")) return true

  return false

getWarnChatEntries(listValue, cacheKey) =>
  if (window[cacheKey] !== undefined) {
    return window[cacheKey]
  }

  const items = listValue?.selectAll
  if (!items) return []

  const entries = []
  for (const item of items) {
    const value = String(
      item.evaluateItem ?? item.getName ?? item ?? ""
    ).trim()
    if (!value || value.startsWith("//")) continue

    const regexMatch = value.match(/^\/([\s\S]*)\/([a-z]*)\s*--\s*(.*)$/i)
    if (!regexMatch) continue

    try {
      entries.push({
        regex: new RegExp(regexMatch[1], regexMatch[2] || "i"),
        reason: regexMatch[3].trim()
      })
    } catch {}
  }

  window[cacheKey] = entries
  return entries

getWarnChatMatch(listValue, cacheKey, values) =>
  const entries = getWarnChatEntries(listValue, cacheKey)

  for (const entry of entries) {
    for (const rawValue of values) {
      const value = String(rawValue || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
      if (!value) continue

      const rx = entry.regex
      if (rx.global || rx.sticky) rx.lastIndex = 0
      if (rx.test(value)) return { reason: entry.reason }
    }
  }

  return null

getWarnChatNameMatch(nickname, username) =>
  return getWarnChatMatch(
    warnChatNames,
    "_warnChatNamesCache",
    [username, nickname]
  )

getWarnChatWordMatch(message) =>
  return getWarnChatMatch(
    warnChatWords,
    "_warnChatWordsCache",
    [message]
  )

buildConceptBoardSaveInfo(data) =>
  const source = data && typeof data === "object" ? data : {}
  const inputs = source.inputs && typeof source.inputs === "object" ? source.inputs : {}
  const meta = source.meta && typeof source.meta === "object" ? source.meta : {}
  const read = (...values) => {
    for (const raw of values) {
      if (raw == null) continue
      const value = typeof raw === "object"
        ? raw.evaluateItem ?? raw.value ?? raw.getName
        : raw
      if (!["string", "number", "boolean"].includes(typeof value)) continue
      const text = String(value).trim()
      if (text && text !== "[object Object]") return text
    }
    return ""
  }
  const prompt = read(source.prompt, inputs.prompt, meta.prompt)
  const promptField = name => {
    const prefix = `(${name}:::`
    const start = prompt.toLowerCase().indexOf(prefix.toLowerCase())
    if (start < 0) return ""
    const valueStart = start + prefix.length
    const end = prompt.indexOf(")", valueStart)
    return end < 0 ? "" : prompt.slice(valueStart, end).trim()
  }
  const formatDate = value => {
    if (value == null || value === "") return new Date().toLocaleString()
    if (typeof value === "string" && !/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(value.trim())) {
      const parsed = new Date(value)
      return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? value.trim() : parsed.toLocaleString()
    }
    const numeric = Number(value)
    const parsed = new Date(numeric < 1e12 ? numeric * 1000 : numeric)
    return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? new Date().toLocaleString() : parsed.toLocaleString()
  }

  const postTitle = read(
    source.postTitle, source.title, source.saveTitle,
    inputs.postTitle, inputs.title, inputs.saveTitle,
    meta.postTitle, meta.title, meta.saveTitle,
    promptField("saveTitle")
  )
  const postDescription = read(
    source.postDescription, source.description, source.saveDescription,
    inputs.postDescription, inputs.saveDescription, inputs.userdescription,
    meta.postDescription, meta.description, meta.saveDescription,
    promptField("saveDescription")
  )
  const style = read(
    source.styleName, source.style, source.artStyleName, source.artStyle,
    inputs.styleName, inputs.style, inputs.artStyleName, inputs.artStyle,
    meta.styleName, meta.style, meta.artStyleName, meta.artStyle
  )
  const negativePrompt = read(source.negativePrompt, inputs.negativePrompt, meta.negativePrompt)
  const seed = source.seed ?? inputs.seed ?? meta.seed
  const guidanceScale = source.guidanceScale ?? inputs.guidanceScale ?? meta.guidanceScale
  const dateValue = read(
    source.createdAt, source.timestamp, source.postedAt, source.date, source.time,
    inputs.createdAt, inputs.timestamp, inputs.date,
    meta.createdAt, meta.timestamp, meta.postedAt, meta.date, meta.time
  )
  const imageUrl = read(source.imageUrl, source.url, meta.imageUrl, meta.url)
  const info = {}

  if (postTitle) info["Post Title"] = postTitle
  if (postDescription) info["Post Description"] = postDescription
  if (style) info.Style = style
  if (source.comment) info.comment = String(source.comment)
  if (prompt) info.prompt = prompt
  if (negativePrompt) info.negativePrompt = negativePrompt
  if (seed != null && String(seed).trim() !== "") info.seed = String(seed)
  if (guidanceScale != null && String(guidanceScale).trim() !== "") info.guidanceScale = String(guidanceScale)
  info["Date / Time"] = formatDate(dateValue)
  if (imageUrl) {
    if (/\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|bmp|svg)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(imageUrl)) info["Image URL"] = imageUrl
    else info.url = imageUrl
  }
  return info
  
getChatPreviewNsfwInfo(rawUrl) =>
  let parsed
  try {
    parsed = new URL(String(rawUrl || "").trim())
  } catch (error) {
    return Promise.resolve({ supported: false, ok: false, isNsfw: false })
  }

  const isUserUpload =
    (parsed.hostname === "user.uploads.dev" ||
    parsed.hostname === "user-uploads.perchance.org") &&
    parsed.pathname.startsWith("/file/")

  const isAigcUpload =
    parsed.hostname === "aigc.uploads.dev" &&
    parsed.pathname.startsWith("/image/")

  if (!isUserUpload && !isAigcUpload) {
    return Promise.resolve({ supported: false, ok: false, isNsfw: false })
  }

  parsed.protocol = "https:"
  if (isUserUpload) parsed.hostname = "user.uploads.dev"
  const normalizedUrl = parsed.href

  return (async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 6000)

    try {
      const infoUrl = isAigcUpload
        ? `https://image-generation.perchance.org/api/imageTags?url=${encodeURIComponent(normalizedUrl)}`
        : `https://upload.perchance.org/api/fileInfo?url=${encodeURIComponent(normalizedUrl)}`

      const response = await fetch(infoUrl, {
        cache: "no-store",
        signal: controller.signal
      })

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Upload metadata request failed: ${response.status}`)
      }

      const result = await response.json()
      if (!result || result.error) {
        throw new Error(result?.error || "Upload metadata request failed.")
      }

      const tags = Array.isArray(result.tags)
        ? result.tags.map(tag => String(tag).toLowerCase())
        : []

      return {
        supported: true,
        ok: true,
        isNsfw: tags.includes("nsfw")
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("Could not verify chat preview upload metadata.", error)
      return { supported: true, ok: false, isNsfw: false }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId)
    }
  })()

// Custom moderation intentionally runs after comments are posted.
// Native bannedUsers/bannedWords remain pre-submit inside the comments plugin.
// Local warnings, yeets, identity checks, and adult nickname checks run in onComment.

targetId(value) =>
  return normalizeChatFullId(value)

normalizeUsernameText(value) =>
  return String(value || "").normalize("NFC").trim().toLowerCase()

normalizeNicknameText(value) =>
  return String(value || "").normalize("NFC").trim()

stampPassport(user) =>
  const visualId = String(user?.visualId || "").trim().toUpperCase()
  const userId = String(user?.id || "").trim().replace(/^id:/i, "").toLowerCase()
  return visualId && userId ? `${visualId}-${userId}` : ""

normalizeTarget(value) =>
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null

  const target = {}
  const fullId = targetId(value.fullId || value.targetIdVal || value.targetFullId)
  const username = String(value.username || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
  const nickname = String(value.nickname || "").normalize("NFC").trim()

  if (fullId) target.fullId = fullId
  if (username) target.username = username
  if (nickname) target.nickname = nickname

  return Object.keys(target).length ? target : null

readTarget(value) =>
  if (!value) return null

  let parsed = value
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    const raw = value.trim()
    if (!raw) return null

    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
    } catch (e) {
      const fullId = targetId(raw)
      return fullId ? { fullId } : null
    }
  }

  if (typeof parsed === "string") {
    const fullId = targetId(parsed)
    return fullId ? { fullId } : null
  }

  return normalizeTarget(parsed)

writeTarget(target) =>
  const normalized = normalizeTarget(target)
  return normalized ? JSON.stringify(normalized) : ""

identityTarget(passportId, nickname, username) =>
  return normalizeTarget({
    fullId: passportId,
    nickname,
    username
  })

// Command target format: [username] (nickname) full-ID.
// Each identifier is optional and independently matchable.
parseTarget(raw) =>
  let rest = String(raw || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
  if (!rest) return null

  const target = {}
  const fullIdMatch = rest.match(/(?:^|\s)([a-z0-9]{4}-[a-z0-9]+)\s*$/i)

  if (fullIdMatch) {
    target.fullId = targetId(fullIdMatch[1])
    rest = rest.slice(0, fullIdMatch.index).trim()
  }

  if (rest.startsWith("[")) {
    const usernameEnd = rest.indexOf("]")
    if (usernameEnd < 1) return null

    const username = rest.slice(1, usernameEnd).normalize("NFC").trim()
    if (!username) return null

    target.username = username
    rest = rest.slice(usernameEnd + 1).trim()
  }

  if (rest) {
    if (!rest.startsWith("(") || !rest.endsWith(")")) return null

    const nickname = rest.slice(1, -1).normalize("NFC").trim()
    if (!nickname) return null

    target.nickname = nickname
    rest = ""
  }

  return rest ? null : normalizeTarget(target)

// Finds the warning reason delimiter only outside [username] and (nickname).
splitModeratorWarningBody(value) =>
  const text = String(value || "")
  let squareDepth = 0
  let roundDepth = 0

  for (let i = 0; i < text.length - 1; i += 1) {
    const char = text[i]

    if (char === "[" && roundDepth === 0) squareDepth += 1
    else if (char === "]" && roundDepth === 0 && squareDepth > 0) squareDepth -= 1
    else if (char === "(" && squareDepth === 0) roundDepth += 1
    else if (char === ")" && squareDepth === 0 && roundDepth > 0) roundDepth -= 1

    if (
      char === "-" &&
      text[i + 1] === "-" &&
      squareDepth === 0 &&
      roundDepth === 0
    ) {
      return {
        targetText: text.slice(0, i).trim(),
        reason: text.slice(i + 2).trim()
      }
    }
  }

  return { targetText: text.trim(), reason: "" }

// Every supplied identifier is an independent OR match.
matchTargetCached(passportId, nickname, username, target) =>
  const normalized = readTarget(target)
  if (!normalized) return false

  if (normalized.fullId && targetId(passportId) === normalized.fullId) return true
  if (
    normalized.username &&
    username &&
    normalizeUsernameText(username) === normalizeUsernameText(normalized.username)
  ) return true
  if (
    normalized.nickname &&
    nickname &&
    normalizeNicknameText(nickname) === normalizeNicknameText(normalized.nickname)
  ) return true

  return false

targetsOverlap(left, right) =>
  const source = readTarget(left)
  const target = readTarget(right)
  if (!source || !target) return false

  return matchTargetCached(
    source.fullId || "",
    source.nickname || "",
    source.username || "",
    target
  )

parseModeratorWarningSpan(value) =>
  const raw = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!raw) {
    return {
      mode: "once",
      span: "",
      remaining: 1,
      expiresAt: 0
    }
  }

  const repeatMatch = raw.match(/^(\d+)x$/i)
  if (repeatMatch) {
    const remaining = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(repeatMatch[1]) || 1, 999999))

    return {
      mode: "repeat",
      span: `${remaining}x`,
      remaining,
      expiresAt: 0
    }
  }

  const timeMatch = raw.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([hdwmy])$/i)
  if (!timeMatch) return null

  const amount = Number(timeMatch[1])
  const unit = timeMatch[2].toLowerCase()
  if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return null

  const unitMs = {
    h: 60 * 60 * 1000,
    d: 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    w: 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    m: 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    y: 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
  }[unit]
  const durationMs = Math.round(amount * unitMs)
  const maxDurationMs = 100 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000

  if (!durationMs || durationMs > maxDurationMs) return null

  return {
    mode: "time",
    span: `${timeMatch[1]}${unit}`,
    remaining: 0,
    expiresAt: Date.now() + durationMs
  }

formatModeratorWarningRemaining(expiresAt) =>
  const remainingMs = Math.max(0, (Number(expiresAt) || 0) - Date.now())
  const units = [
    ["year", 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000],
    ["month", 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000],
    ["week", 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000],
    ["day", 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000],
    ["hour", 60 * 60 * 1000],
    ["minute", 60 * 1000]
  ]

  for (const [label, unitMs] of units) {
    if (remainingMs < unitMs) continue

    const rawAmount = remainingMs / unitMs
    const amount = rawAmount < 10
      ? Math.max(0.1, Math.floor(rawAmount * 10) / 10)
      : Math.floor(rawAmount)
    const displayAmount = Number.isInteger(amount) ? amount : amount.toFixed(1)

    return `${displayAmount} ${label}${amount === 1 ? "" : "s"} remaining`
  }

  return "Less than 1 minute remaining"

parseModeratorWarningCommand(message) =>
  const body = String(message || "").replace(/^@warn\b\s*/i, "").trim()
  const spanMatch = body.match(/\s*\[span:\s*([^\]]+?)\s*\]\s*$/i)
  const parsedSpan = parseModeratorWarningSpan(spanMatch?.[1] || "")
  if (spanMatch && !parsedSpan) return null

  const withoutSpan = spanMatch
    ? body.slice(0, spanMatch.index).trim()
    : body
  const parts = splitModeratorWarningBody(withoutSpan)
  const target = parseTarget(parts.targetText)
  if (!target) return null

  return {
    target,
    reason: parts.reason.slice(0, 500),
    ...parsedSpan
  }

formatModeratorTarget(target) =>
  const normalized = readTarget(target)
  if (!normalized) return "Unknown target"

  return [
    normalized.username ? `[${normalized.username}]` : "",
    normalized.nickname ? `(${normalized.nickname})` : "",
    normalized.fullId || ""
  ].filter(Boolean).join(" ")

showLocalModeratorCommandResult(title, lines) =>
  const details = (Array.isArray(lines) ? lines : [lines])
    .map(line => String(line || "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean)

  if (typeof window.showSubNotice === "function") {
    window.showSubNotice(title, details)
    return
  }

  if (typeof notifyRefreshManual === "function") {
    notifyRefreshManual(
      `<i class="fa-solid fa-shield-halved" style="margin-right:6px;"></i>${title}`,
      details.join("\n") || null,
      {
        showRefresh: false,
        singletonId: "moderatorCommandResult"
      }
    )
  }

showBailReleaseNotice() =>
  const title = "Moderator bail applied"
  const details = [
    "Your active moderation restriction has been cleared.",
    "You may continue using the site. Normal rules still apply."
  ]

  if (typeof window.showSubNotice === "function") {
    window.showSubNotice(title, details)
    return
  }

  if (typeof notifyRefreshManual === "function") {
    notifyRefreshManual(
      `<i class="fa-solid fa-unlock-keyhole" style="margin-right:6px;"></i>${title}`,
      details.join("\n"),
      {
        showRefresh: false,
        singletonId: "moderatorBailRelease"
      }
    )
  }

readModeratorWarning() =>
  let warning = null

  try {
    warning = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("_moderator_warning_v2") || "null")
  } catch (e) {}

  localStorage.removeItem("_moderator_warning_v1")

  const target = readTarget(
    warning?.target || warning?.targetIdVal || warning?.targetFullId
  )
  const mode = ["once", "repeat", "time"].includes(warning?.mode)
    ? warning.mode
    : "once"
  const remaining = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(warning?.remaining) || 0))
  const expiresAt = Number(warning?.expiresAt) || 0

  const isInvalid =
    !target ||
    (mode === "once" && remaining <= 0) ||
    (mode === "repeat" && remaining <= 0) ||
    (mode === "time" && (!expiresAt || expiresAt <= Date.now()))

  if (isInvalid) {
    localStorage.removeItem("_moderator_warning_v2")
    return null
  }

  return {
    target,
    reason: String(warning?.reason || "").slice(0, 500),
    mode,
    span: String(warning?.span || "").slice(0, 30),
    remaining,
    expiresAt
  }

clearModeratorWarning() =>
  localStorage.removeItem("_moderator_warning_v1")
  localStorage.removeItem("_moderator_warning_v2")

  const banner = document.getElementById("moderatorWarningBanner")
  if (banner) banner.remove()

showModeratorWarning(warning) =>
  if (!warning || !document.body) return

  const escapeHtml = value => String(value || "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({
    "&": "&amp;",
    "<": "&lt;",
    ">": "&gt;",
    '"': "&quot;",
    "'": "&#39;"
  })[char])

  const details = []

  if (warning.reason) {
    details.push(`Reason: ${escapeHtml(warning.reason)}`)
  }

  if (warning.mode === "time") {
    details.push(escapeHtml(formatModeratorWarningRemaining(warning.expiresAt)))
  } else if (warning.mode === "repeat") {
    const displayNumber = Math.max(1, warning.remaining)
    details.push(`${displayNumber.toLocaleString()} ${displayNumber === 1 ? "time" : "times"} remaining`)
  }

  notifyRefreshManual(
    "<i class=\"fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation\" style=\"margin-right:6px;\"></i>You've been warned.",
    details.join("\n") || null,
    {
      variant: "warning",
      showRefresh: false,
      singletonId: "moderatorWarningBanner",
      targetIdVal: warning.target?.fullId || ""
    }
  )

activateStoredModeratorWarning(passportId, nickname, username) =>
  const warning = readModeratorWarning()
  if (!warning) return false

  if (!matchTargetCached(passportId, nickname, username, warning.target)) {
    return false
  }

  showModeratorWarning(warning)

  if (warning.mode !== "time") {
    warning.remaining -= 1

    if (warning.remaining > 0) {
      localStorage.setItem("_moderator_warning_v2", JSON.stringify(warning))
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem("_moderator_warning_v2")
    }
  }

  return true

baseOnComment(comment, adultChannel, submitScope) =>
  const yeetWords = getChatYeetWords()
  const message = String(comment.message || "")
  const normalizedMessage = message.normalize("NFC")

  // Moderator-only targeted moderation commands.
  if (comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    // Manual mercy: clear current negative state and accept both adult-room rule notices.
    // The permanent minor-age ban is intentionally not cleared.
    if (/^@bail\b/i.test(normalizedMessage)) {
      const raw = message.replace(/^@bail\b\s*/i, "").trim()
      const target = parseTarget(raw)

      if (target) {
        const passportId = window._passportId || localStorage.getItem("_passportId") || ""
        const nickname = window._myNickname || localStorage.getItem("_myNickname") || ""
        const username = window._myUsername || localStorage.getItem("_myUsername") || ""
        const storedCurse = localStorage.getItem("_yeet_curse")
        const stored3dCurse = localStorage.getItem("_3d_curse")
        const storedWarning = readModeratorWarning()
        const isLocalTarget =
          matchTargetCached(passportId, nickname, username, target) ||
          targetsOverlap(storedCurse, target) ||
          targetsOverlap(stored3dCurse, target) ||
          targetsOverlap(storedWarning?.target, target)

        if (targetsOverlap(storedCurse, target) || isLocalTarget) {
          localStorage.removeItem("_yeet_curse")
        }

        if (targetsOverlap(stored3dCurse, target) || isLocalTarget) {
          localStorage.removeItem("_3d_curse")
        }

        if (
          window._warnTarget?.target &&
          targetsOverlap(window._warnTarget.target, target)
        ) {
          window._warnTarget = null
        }

        if (
          storedWarning?.target &&
          (
            targetsOverlap(storedWarning.target, target) ||
            isLocalTarget
          )
        ) {
          clearModeratorWarning()
        }

        if (isLocalTarget && !localStorage.getItem("_adultMinorPermanentBanV1")) {
          // @bail is an active release: clear persistent moderation state AND
          // dismantle live targeted moderation UI/listeners immediately. No refresh needed.
          clearChatYeet()
          clearChat3d()
          localStorage.removeItem("_adultNicknameCorrectionRequiredV1")
          writeAdultRuleConsent("chat2", "bail")
          writeAdultRuleConsent("adult-lounge", "bail")
          setAdultWaitingRoomStatus(
            "ready",
            "Moderator @bail cleared the active moderation state. Keep a clear adult age in your nickname to post."
          )
          showBailReleaseNotice()
        }

        if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
          showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Bail command accepted", [
            `Target: ${formatModeratorTarget(target)}`,
            "Matching yeets, 3D overlays, age/word curses, and warnings are cleared immediately; no refresh is required.",
            "Permanent no-appeal minor-age bans are not cleared."
          ])
        }
      } else if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
        showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Bail command not accepted", [
          "Use @bail [username] (nickname) full-ID.",
          "Username, nickname, and full ID are optional independent OR targets, but at least one is required."
        ])
      }
    }

    // A new warning replaces the pending warning state for its target.
    if (/^@warn\b/i.test(normalizedMessage)) {
      const warning = parseModeratorWarningCommand(message)

      if (warning) {
        window._warnTarget = warning

        if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
          const spanText = warning.mode === "time"
            ? `Duration: ${warning.span}`
            : warning.mode === "repeat"
              ? `Displays: ${warning.remaining}`
              : "Displays: once"

          showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Warning queued", [
            `Target: ${formatModeratorTarget(warning.target)}`,
            warning.reason ? `Reason: ${warning.reason}` : "Reason: none",
            spanText
          ])
        }
      } else if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
        showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Warning not queued", [
          "Use @warn [username] (nickname) full-ID -- reason [span:3x].",
          "The target is required. Span is optional and accepts Nx, h, d, w, m, or y."
        ])
      }
    }

    // @3d fires immediately on the matching browser and persists until @bail.
    if (/^@3d\b/i.test(normalizedMessage)) {
      const raw = message.replace(/^@3d\b\s*/i, "").trim()
      const target = parseTarget(raw)

      if (target) {
        const localPassportId = window._passportId || localStorage.getItem("_passportId") || ""
        const localNickname = window._myNickname || localStorage.getItem("_myNickname") || ""
        const localUsername = window._myUsername || localStorage.getItem("_myUsername") || ""
        const isLocalTarget = matchTargetCached(
          localPassportId,
          localNickname,
          localUsername,
          target
        )

        if (isLocalTarget) {
          const localIdentity = identityTarget(localPassportId, localNickname, localUsername)
          // A newly fired @3d always starts at the fake-close stage.
          localStorage.removeItem("_3d_fullscreen_stage")
          localStorage.setItem("_3d_curse", writeTarget(localIdentity || target))
          chat3d()
        }

        if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
          showLocalModeratorCommandResult("3D fired", [
            `Target: ${formatModeratorTarget(target)}`,
            "Matching users receive the sticky 3D prank; its top-right ✕ enters browser fullscreen, then disappears until @bail."
          ])
        }
      } else if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
        showLocalModeratorCommandResult("3D not fired", [
          "Use @3d [username] (nickname) full-ID.",
          "Username, nickname, and full ID are optional independent OR targets, but at least one is required."
        ])
      }
    }

    // @yeet fires once, immediately, on the matching browser.
    if (/^@yeet\b/i.test(normalizedMessage)) {
      const raw = message.replace(/^@yeet\b\s*/i, "").trim()
      const target = parseTarget(raw)

      if (target) {
        const localPassportId = window._passportId || localStorage.getItem("_passportId") || ""
        const localNickname = window._myNickname || localStorage.getItem("_myNickname") || ""
        const localUsername = window._myUsername || localStorage.getItem("_myUsername") || ""
        const isLocalTarget = matchTargetCached(
          localPassportId,
          localNickname,
          localUsername,
          target
        )

        if (isLocalTarget) {
          const localIdentity = identityTarget(localPassportId, localNickname, localUsername)
          localStorage.setItem("_yeet_curse", writeTarget(localIdentity || target))
          chatYeet()
        }

        if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
          showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Yeet fired", [
            `Target: ${formatModeratorTarget(target)}`,
            "Matching users are ejected immediately when their browser receives this command."
          ])
        }
      } else if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
        showLocalModeratorCommandResult("Yeet not fired", [
          "Use @yeet [username] (nickname) full-ID.",
          "Username, nickname, and full ID are optional independent OR targets, but at least one is required."
        ])
      }
    }
  }

  // Only this browser's own comment can reveal and match its full identity.
  if (comment.byCurrentUser) {
    const passportId = stampPassport(comment.user)
    if (passportId) {
      window._passportId = passportId
      localStorage.setItem("_passportId", passportId)
    }

    const u = comment.user || {}
    const nick = normalizePg13ProfileValue(u.nickname || u.nick)
    const username = normalizePg13ProfileValue(u.name)
    const avatar = String(u.avatarUrl || u.avatar || "").trim()
    window._myNickname = nick
    localStorage.setItem("_myNickname", nick)
    window._myUsername = username
    localStorage.setItem("_myUsername", username)
    window._myAvatar = avatar
    localStorage.setItem("_myAvatar", avatar)
    window._chatIdentityHydratedFromComments = true

    const currentIdentity = identityTarget(passportId, nick, username)

    // This is the authoritative custom moderation path because it sees the real posted identity.
    if (localStorage.getItem("_adultMinorPermanentBanV1")) {
      chatMinorPermanentBan()
      return
    }

    if (readTarget(localStorage.getItem("_yeet_curse"))) {
      chatYeet()
      return
    }

    // Re-check exact ID and nickname/username lists against the real posted profile.
    if (
      (passportId && isUserBannedByFullId(passportId)) ||
      isUserBannedByNicknameOrUsername(nick, username)
    ) {
      if (currentIdentity) {
        localStorage.setItem("_yeet_curse", writeTarget(currentIdentity))
      }
      chatYeet()
      return
    }

      comment.user?.isAdmin &&
      /^@(3d|warn|yeet|bail|all|pin|news|update|refresh|hardrefresh|ban)\b/i.test(normalizedMessage)

    const wordWarn = isModeratorCommand
      ? null
      : getWarnChatWordMatch(message)
    const automaticWarnReason = [...new Set([
      nameWarn?.reason,
      wordWarn?.reason
    ].map(value => String(value || "").trim()).filter(Boolean))].join(" ")

    if (automaticWarnReason) {
      showModeratorWarning({
        target: currentIdentity || { fullId: passportId },
        reason: automaticWarnReason.slice(0, 500),
        mode: "once",
        remaining: 1,
        expiresAt: 0
      })
    }

    if (passportId) {
      const activeWarning = window._warnTarget
      if (activeWarning?.target) {
        if (matchTargetCached(passportId, nick, username, activeWarning.target)) {
          localStorage.setItem(
            "_moderator_warning_v2",
            JSON.stringify({
              ...activeWarning,
              target: identityTarget(passportId, nick, username)
            })
          )
            }

        window._warnTarget = null
      }

      activateStoredModeratorWarning(passportId, nick, username)
    }

    if (!adultChannel && (submitScope === "chat" || submitScope === "gallery")) {
      if (avatar || nick || username) {
        localStorage.removeItem("_pg13ProfileSetupRequiredV1")
      } else {
        // The comments plugin reveals the authoritative profile only after a post.
        // Let that first true-anon PG-13 message land, then require setup thereafter.
        localStorage.setItem("_pg13ProfileSetupRequiredV1", "1")
        showPg13ProfileSetupRoom(submitScope)
      }
    }

    // Adult rooms verify the actual posted nickname after the one allowed post.
    if (adultChannel) {
      const ageResult = classifyAdultNickname(nick)

      if (ageResult.status === "minor") {
        applyAdultMinorPermanentBan(currentIdentity, ageResult.age)
        return
      }

      if (ageResult.status === "adult") {
        localStorage.removeItem("_adultNicknameCorrectionRequiredV1")
      } else {
        localStorage.setItem("_adultNicknameCorrectionRequiredV1", "1")
        showAdultNicknameCheck(adultChannel, ageResult.status)
      }
    }
  }

  if (comment.byCurrentUser && yeetWords.some(rx => {
    if (rx.global || rx.sticky) rx.lastIndex = 0
    return rx.test(message)
  })) {
    const user = comment.user || {}
    const currentIdentity = identityTarget(
      stampPassport(user),
      String(user.nickname || user.nick || "").normalize("NFC").trim(),
      String(user.name || "").normalize("NFC").trim()
    )

    if (currentIdentity) {
      localStorage.setItem("_yeet_curse", writeTarget(currentIdentity))
    }

    chatYeet()
    return
  }
  
  // 1. --- SILENT AUTO-SAVE & INFO GATHERING ---
  const isSpoilerMsg = /(?:^|\s)\/(?:spoiler|sp|cw)\b/i.test(comment.message);
  const urlMatch = comment.message.match(/https?:\/\/\S+/i);
  const foundUrl = urlMatch ? urlMatch[0] : null;

  let infoData = null;

  if (foundUrl) {
    const u = comment.user || {};
    const senderVisualId = String(u.visualId || "ANON").toUpperCase();
    const senderName = u.name || "Anonymous";
    const senderNickname = u.nickname || u.nick || "";
    
    // Format: username (nickname) — parenthesis only appear if nickname exists
    const nameDisplay = senderNickname ? `${senderName} (${senderNickname})` : senderName;

    // The sleek, faithful comment format: 👤[ID] username (nickname)
    const formattedComment = `👤[${senderVisualId}] ${nameDisplay}\n💬 ${comment.message || ""}`;

    infoData = buildConceptBoardSaveInfo({
      imageUrl: foundUrl,
      comment: formattedComment,
      timestamp: comment.timestamp ?? comment.createdAt ?? comment.date
    });

    const shouldAutoSave = window.shouldAutoSaveChatLink
      ? window.shouldAutoSaveChatLink(comment.user)
      : localStorage.getItem("autoSaveLinks") === "true";

    if (shouldAutoSave) {
      silentSaveToConceptBoard(foundUrl, infoData);
    }
  }

  if (/^@hardrefresh\b/i.test(comment.message) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    const commandId = String(comment.id || comment.timestamp || comment.createdAt || normalizedMessage)
    const hrKey = "hr_cmd_" + commandId

    if (!localStorage.getItem(hrKey)) {
      localStorage.setItem(hrKey, Date.now().toString())

      const customMessage = comment.message.replace(/^@hardrefresh\b\s*/i, "").trim()
      const defaultReason = "⚠️ Save work immediately. Due to mass drama/troll raid, we need to do this."
      triggerHardRefresh("🛑 HARD REFRESH INITIATED", customMessage || defaultReason)
    }
  }

  // --- TROLL LINK INTERCEPTOR ---
  if(comment.byCurrentUser&&/(secret\.viralsachxd\.com|perchance\.org\/secret|secretmessage\.net|secretmessage\.link|perchance\.org\/furpost)/i.test(comment.message)){
    if(localStorage.getItem("hideTrollPopup")!=="true"){
      const wrap=document.createElement("div");
      wrap.className="dim-overlay-troll";
      wrap.innerHTML=`
        <style>
          .dim-overlay-troll{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:100006;background:rgba(0,0,0,.86);backdrop-filter:blur(5px);display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;padding:20px}
          .troll-popup-stack{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:10px;max-width:96vw;max-height:92vh}
          .troll-popup-close{position:fixed;top:14px;right:14px;width:42px;height:42px;border:none;border-radius:12px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.38);color:#fff;font-size:28px;line-height:1;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;backdrop-filter:blur(4px);box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.35)}
          .troll-popup-close:hover{background:rgba(255,255,255,.12)}
          .troll-popup-image{display:block;max-width:min(96vw,1200px);max-height:calc(92vh - 42px);width:auto;height:auto;object-fit:contain;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 14px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.45)}
          .troll-popup-footer{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;color:#eee;font:500 14px/1.2 ui-sans-serif,system-ui,-apple-system;user-select:none;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap}
          .troll-popup-footer input[type="checkbox"]{accent-color:var(--special-color,#8ab4f8);transform:scale(1.12);cursor:pointer;margin:0}
        </style>
        <button class="troll-popup-close" type="button" aria-label="Close">×</button>
        <div class="troll-popup-stack">
          <img class="troll-popup-image" src="https://user.uploads.dev/file/ce5194b8a9c67c3216ff53ae9275be4f.webp" alt="Troll Room">
          <label class="troll-popup-footer">
            <input type="checkbox" onchange="localStorage.setItem('hideTrollPopup',this.checked?'true':'false')">
            Never show again
          </label>
        </div>
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(wrap);
      wrap.addEventListener("click",()=>wrap.remove());
      wrap.querySelector(".troll-popup-close")?.addEventListener("click",e=>{e.stopPropagation();wrap.remove()});
      wrap.querySelector(".troll-popup-stack")?.addEventListener("click",e=>e.stopPropagation());
    }
  }
  
  // 2. --- NOTIFICATION KILL SWITCH ---
  if (window?.perchNotifyToggle === false) return
  
  const allowTopText = window?.perchTextAlertsToggle !== false;
  const allowLeftLinks = window?.perchLinkPreviewToggle !== false;
  const allowAutoRefresh = window?.perchForceRefreshToggle === true;
  const allowActionNotif = allowTopText;
  const allowSpecialFx = allowTopText && allowLeftLinks;
  
  if (allowTopText && /[:_].*?awo+.*?[:_]/i.test(comment.message)) notify("Awoo!");
  if (allowTopText && comment.byCurrentUser && /(:\w*_deadchat:|\bdead\s*chat\b|deadchatxd|Dead[_]?chat|pepedeadchat|PepeDeadChat|DeadChat1)/i.test(comment.message)) notify("indeed.");
  if (allowTopText && /(^|\W)ban[\s\-]?rain(\W|$)/i.test(comment.message) && comment.user?.isAdmin) banRain();
  
  // --- SHOW POP-UPS ---

  const hideLinkPreview =
    window.shouldHideChatLinkPreview?.(comment?.user || null) === true;

  if (allowLeftLinks && !hideLinkPreview) {
    if (isSpoilerMsg && foundUrl) {
      notifyWithLink("🕶️ Spoiler link hidden", foundUrl, true, comment?.user || null, infoData, false)
    } else if (foundUrl) {
      getChatPreviewNsfwInfo(foundUrl)
        .then(previewInfo => {
          const isAutoFlagged =
            previewInfo?.supported === true &&
            previewInfo?.ok === true &&
            previewInfo?.isNsfw === true

          notifyWithLink(
            isAutoFlagged
              ? "🔞 Auto-flagged Perchance upload"
              : `⚠️ Link detected: ${foundUrl}`,
            foundUrl,
            false,
            comment?.user || null,
            infoData,
            isAutoFlagged
          )
        })
        .catch(error => {
          console.warn("Chat preview metadata check failed.", error)
          notifyWithLink(`⚠️ Link detected: ${foundUrl}`, foundUrl, false, comment?.user || null, infoData, false)
        })
    }
  } // link preview pop up

  if (allowTopText && /^@all\b/i.test(comment.message) && comment.user?.isAdmin)
    notify("🔔 𝗔𝗻𝗻𝗼𝘂𝗻𝗰𝗲𝗺𝗲𝗻𝘁:\n" + comment.message.replace(/^@all\b/i, '').trim())

  if (allowActionNotif && /^@pin\b/i.test(comment.message) && comment.user?.isAdmin)
    notifyPinned(comment.message.replace(/^@pin\b\s*/i, "").trim())

  if (/^@news\b/i.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    const raw = comment.message.replace(/^@news\b\s*/i, "").trim();
    const main = "⚡ New feature live! Check #Announcement channel.";
  
    if (allowActionNotif) {
      notifyRefreshManual(main + "<br>Refresh to apply when you’re ready.", raw || null);
    }
  }
  
  if (/^@update\b/i.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    const raw = comment.message.replace(/^@update\b\s*/i, "").trim();
    const main = "⚡ Site updated.";
  
    if (allowActionNotif) {
      notifyRefreshManual(main + " Please refresh soon to use the latest version.", raw || null);
    }
  }

  const doMention = (regex, title, isPinned = false) => {
    if (!allowTopText) return;

    const m = comment.message.match(new RegExp(regex.source + "([\\s\\S]*)", "i"));
    if (m) {
      const subMsg = m[1].trim() || null;
      if (isPinned) {
        notifyPinned(title, subMsg);
      } else {
        notify(title, subMsg);
      }
    }
  };
  
  doMention(/@mod(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Someone requested a moderator", true); // <-- Added 'true' to make it a pin!
  doMention(/@(?:gh0st1|ghost|snep)(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator 🌜Ghost🌛 summoned!");
  doMention(/@fennec(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Fennec summoned!");
  doMention(/@rud(?:bo)?(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator RudBo 🐯 summoned!");
  doMention(/@glass(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator glass (Cowboy Cat) summoned!");
  doMention(/@offline(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator OFFLINE (𓁹𓂀𓁹) summoned!");
  doMention(/@wrench(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Wrench summoned!");
  doMention(/@kah(?:ru)?(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Kahru summoned!");
  doMention(/@(?:yiffgpt|kendal)(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Kendal summoned!");
  doMention(/@(?:fuzzygrmzl|fuzzy)(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Fuzzy Graymuzzle summoned!");
  doMention(/@zodd(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Zodd — your planet needs you!");
  doMention(/@mellow(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Mellow summoned!");
  doMention(/@(?:smolkobold|sirkobold|iheartkobolds)(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator iHeartKobolds summoned!");
  doMention(/@(?:cheese|colli|collie)(?:\s|$)/i, "📢 Moderator Cheese The Collie summoned!");
  doMention(/@(?:blue\s*wolf|ryu)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User Ryu (BlueWolf) mentioned");
  doMention(/@(?:nursewolfy|nurse|wolfy)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User Please see chat NurseWolfy");
  doMention(/@gouki(?:666)?(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User Gouki666 (豪鬼) mentioned");
  doMention(/@perp(?:lexity)?(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User perplexity mentioned");
  doMention(/@(?:tdo|the\s*decent\s*one)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User The Decent One mentioned");
  doMention(/@veg(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User veg mentioned");
  doMention(/@(?:awolf1|awkward)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User Awkward_Wolf1 mentioned");
  doMention(/@caz(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User caz mentioned");

  doMention(/@(?:indi|indigirl)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User IndiGirl mentioned");
  doMention(/@(?:FoxyBoy|FluffyFox)(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User FoxyBoy mentioned");

  doMention(/@(?:lionsmane1|lionsmane)(?=[\s.,!?;:)]|$)/i, "👤 LionsMane Summoned");

  doMention(/@Howly(?:\s|$)/i, "👤 User Howly mentioned");
  
  // Do not early-return here.
  // "Top text only" must still allow top text,
  // and "Left links only" must still allow left previews.
  
  // holiday animation 
  // holiday animation 
  
  // moderations
  if (allowTopText && /\blast warning[\s.!?]*$/i.test(comment.message) && comment.user?.isAdmin)
    notify("⚠️ LAST WARNING!\nSomeone has been warned. Follow the rules.");

  if (allowTopText && /:WARNING_column1::WARNING_column2::WARNING_column3::WARNING_column4::WARNING_column5:/m.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin)
    notify("⚠️ WARNING!\nSomeone has been warned. Follow the rules.");

  if (/You(?:’|')re entering a :181: chat\./m.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC"))) {
    if (allowAutoRefresh) triggerAgeConfirmTimer("Time left for that new user to confirm age.");
    if (allowTopText) notify("⚠️ 𝗔𝗧𝗧𝗘𝗡𝗧𝗜𝗢𝗡: A new user has entered the 🔞 chat.\nIf 𝘆𝗼𝘂 𝗮𝗿𝗲 𝘁𝗵𝗮𝘁 𝘂𝘀𝗲𝗿, please 𝗰𝗼𝗻𝘁𝗮𝗰𝘁 𝗮 𝗺𝗼𝗱𝗲𝗿𝗮𝘁𝗼𝗿 𝗻𝗼𝘄 to confirm your age.");
  }

  if (/^@ban\b/i.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    const reasonRaw = comment.message.replace(/^@ban\b\s*/i, "").trim();
    const main = "🛑 A user has been banned. Refresh to update your chat and gallery view.";
    const reason = reasonRaw ? "<b>Ban note:</b> " + reasonRaw : null;
  
    if (allowActionNotif) {
      notifyRefreshManual(main, reason);
    }
  }

  // Auto refresh
  if (allowTopText && comment.byCurrentUser && /^\/testrefresh\b/i.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC"))) {
    const msg = comment.message.replace(/^\/testrefresh\b\s*/i, "").trim();
    triggerGlobalRefresh(
      "🧪 Test Refresh — this demo is only visible to you, and the page will reload after the countdown.",
      msg || `<b>Test Refresh</b><br>
      Simulates the moderator refresh popup after site updates.<br>
      Pause with <i class='fa-regular fa-circle-pause'></i> or toggle via 
      <button style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:1.3em;height:1.3em;border:none;border-radius:0.3em;background:var(--special-color);color:#fff;margin:0 0.15em;'>
        <span class='material-symbols-outlined' style='font-size:1em;'>chrome_reader_mode</span>
      </button>
      <button style='display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:1.3em;height:1.3em;border:none;border-radius:0.3em;background:var(--special-color);color:#fff;margin:0 0.15em;'>
        <span class='material-symbols-outlined' style='font-size:1em;'>notifications_active</span>
      </button>.<br>
      You can also add your own message, e.g. <code>/testrefresh My first test!</code><br>
      Page refreshes after the countdown.`
    );
  }
  
  if (allowActionNotif && /^@refresh\b/i.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    const customMessage = comment.message.replace(/^@refresh\b\s*/i, "").trim();
  
    if (allowAutoRefresh) {
      triggerGlobalRefresh("⟳︎ Refresh to apply updates.", customMessage || null);
    } else {
      notifyRefreshManual("⟳︎ Refresh to apply updates.", customMessage || null);
    }
  }
  
  if (allowActionNotif && /:IGNORE_column1::IGNORE_column2::IGNORE_column3::IGNORE_column4:/m.test(comment.message.normalize("NFC")) && comment.user?.isAdmin) {
    if (allowAutoRefresh) {
      triggerGlobalRefresh("⊘ IGNORE! Troll has been banned. ⟳︎ Refresh and move on.");
    } else {
      notifyRefreshManual("⊘ IGNORE! Troll has been banned.", "Refresh manually when you're ready.");
    }
  }

banRain() =>
  (() => {
    const d=document
    if(!d.getElementById("banCSS")){
      const s=d.createElement("style")
      s.id="banCSS"
      s.textContent=`
        @keyframes banFall {
          0% {
            transform: translateY(-100px) translateX(var(--drift)) rotate(var(--tilt));
            opacity: 0;
          }
          10% { opacity: 0.95; }
          90% { opacity: 0.95; }
          100% {
            transform: translateY(calc(100vh - 40px)) translateX(var(--drift)) rotate(var(--tilt));
            opacity: 0.95;
          }
        }
        @keyframes banHang {
          from { transform: translateY(calc(100vh - 40px)) translateX(var(--drift)) rotate(var(--tilt)); opacity: 0.95; }
          to   { transform: translateY(calc(100vh - 40px)) translateX(var(--drift)) rotate(var(--tilt)); opacity: 0.95; }
        }
        @keyframes banFade { from {opacity:0.95;} to {opacity:0;} }

        .ban {
          top:0; left:0; position:fixed;
          pointer-events:none; z-index:99999;
          will-change:transform,opacity; opacity:0;
          animation:
            banFall var(--fly) ease-in var(--delay) forwards,
            banHang var(--dwell) linear calc(var(--delay) + var(--fly)) forwards,
            banFade var(--fade) ease-in calc(var(--delay) + var(--fly) + var(--dwell)) forwards;
        }
        .ban img { display:block; width:var(--size); transform:rotate(var(--tilt)); }
      `
      d.head.appendChild(s)
    }

    const urls=[
      "https://user.uploads.dev/file/dbaa6c888365dc659ca55f1033b4a818.webp",
      "https://user.uploads.dev/file/d6a277f08caa116e7f83ed05ad7bd32c.webp",
      "https://user.uploads.dev/file/ba89f7f9f6a773ef9604891e6a935ffd.webp",
      "https://user.uploads.dev/file/2a886f0e3bf95962d3706248a6dd2541.webp",
      "https://user.uploads.dev/file/3bf04723cb3b1ccd3a7bc36ad298cc4d.webp"
    ]

    for(let i=0;i<20;i++){
      const el=d.createElement("div"); el.className="ban"
      const size=(24+Math.random()*8|0)+"px"
      const fly =(1.1+Math.random()*2.1)
      const dwell=(1.2+Math.random()*0.6)
      const fade=(0.6+Math.random()*0.6)
      const delay=(Math.random()*3)
      const drift=(Math.random()*30-15).toFixed(1)+"vw"
      const tilt =(Math.random()*60-30).toFixed(1)+"deg"
      el.style.setProperty("--size",size)
      el.style.setProperty("--fly",fly+"s")
      el.style.setProperty("--dwell",dwell+"s")
      el.style.setProperty("--fade",fade+"s")
      el.style.setProperty("--delay",delay+"s")
      el.style.setProperty("--drift",drift)
      el.style.setProperty("--tilt",tilt)
      el.style.left=(Math.random()*100).toFixed(1)+"vw"
      el.innerHTML=`<img src="${urls[(Math.random()*urls.length)|0]}">`
      d.body.appendChild(el)
      setTimeout(()=>el.remove(), (delay+fly+dwell+fade)*1000+120)
    }
  })()

        transform: translate3d(calc(var(--x) + var(--dx)), 105vh, 0) rotate(calc(var(--r) + var(--spin)));
      }

      }

prideFxRun(content, urls, count, minSize, sizeSpread) =>
  const now = Date.now()
  if (window.prideFxLastRun && now - window.prideFxLastRun < 1200) return
  window.prideFxLastRun = now

  prideFxEnsureStyle()

  const oldLayer = document.getElementById("prideFxLayer")
  if (oldLayer) oldLayer.remove()

  if (window.prideFxTimer) clearTimeout(window.prideFxTimer)

  const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches
  const isMobile = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(max-width: 700px)").matches
  const safeCount = reduceMotion ? 8 : isMobile ? Math.min(count, 18) : count

  const layer = document.createElement("div")
  layer.id = "prideFxLayer"
  layer.className = "pride-fx-layer"

  const frag = document.createDocumentFragment()

  for (let i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement("div")
    const size = minSize + Math.random() * sizeSpread
    const duration = 3600 + Math.random() * 2200
    const delay = Math.random() * 1200
    const x = Math.random() * 100
    const drift = Math.random() * 26 - 13
    const rotate = Math.random() * 60 - 30
    const spin = Math.random() * 120 - 60

    el.className = "pride-fx-piece"
    el.style.cssText = `width:${size}px;--x:${x}vw;--dx:${drift}vw;--r:${rotate}deg;--spin:${spin}deg;--dur:${duration}ms;--delay:${delay}ms;--o:.95;`

    if (urls && urls.length) {
      const img = document.createElement("img")
      img.src = urls[Math.floor(Math.random() * urls.length)]
      img.alt = ""
      img.draggable = false
      img.decoding = "async"
      el.appendChild(img)
    } else {
      el.textContent = content
      el.style.fontSize = `${size}px`
    }

    frag.appendChild(el)
  }

  layer.appendChild(frag)
  document.body.appendChild(layer)

  window.prideFxTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    layer.remove()
    window.prideFxTimer = null
  }, 7800)

clearChat3d() =>
  localStorage.removeItem("_3d_curse")
  localStorage.removeItem("_3d_fullscreen_stage")

  const overlay = document.getElementById("chat3dOverlay")
  if (overlay) overlay.remove()

  if (window._chat3dOwnsFullscreen && document.fullscreenElement) {
    document.exitFullscreen?.().catch(() => {})
  }
  window._chat3dOwnsFullscreen = false

chat3d() =>
  if (document.getElementById("chat3dOverlay")) return

  const fullscreenStage = localStorage.getItem("_3d_fullscreen_stage") === "locked"
  const wrap = document.createElement("div")
  wrap.id = "chat3dOverlay"
  wrap.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true")
  wrap.innerHTML = `<style>#chat3dOverlay{inset:0;width:100vw;height:100dvh;position:fixed;z-index:2147483647;overflow:hidden;background:#000;pointer-events:auto;touch-action:none;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none}#chat3dOverlay img{width:100%;height:100%;display:block;object-fit:cover;pointer-events:none;user-select:none;-webkit-user-drag:none}#chat3dEnterFullscreen{top:18px;right:26px;width:32px;height:32px;position:absolute;z-index:2;display:${fullscreenStage ? "none" : "flex"};align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:0;border:0;border-radius:50%;background:#000;color:#fff;font:400 22px/1 Arial,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 1px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.45);-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}#chat3dEnterFullscreen:hover{background:#111}#chat3dEnterFullscreen:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #fff;outline-offset:2px}</style><img src="https://user.uploads.dev/file/c5ed0be505eb7ebe2f72ee9dceb0c556.webp" alt="" draggable="false"><button id="chat3dEnterFullscreen" type="button" aria-label="Close" title="Close">×</button>`
  document.body.appendChild(wrap)

  wrap.addEventListener("contextmenu", event => event.preventDefault())

  const enterButton = document.getElementById("chat3dEnterFullscreen")
  if (enterButton && !fullscreenStage) {
    enterButton.addEventListener("click", async event => {
      event.preventDefault()
      event.stopPropagation()

      // The apparent dismiss action commits stage two first, so the control
      // stays gone across refreshes and fullscreen transitions until @bail.
      localStorage.setItem("_3d_fullscreen_stage", "locked")
      enterButton.remove()

      if (document.fullscreenElement || !document.documentElement.requestFullscreen) return

      try {
        await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen({ navigationUI: "hide" })
        window._chat3dOwnsFullscreen = document.fullscreenElement === document.documentElement
      } catch (error) {}
    }, { once: true })
  }

clearChatYeet() =>
  localStorage.removeItem("_yeet_curse")

  const overlay = document.getElementById("chatYeetOverlay")
  if (overlay) overlay.remove()

  if (window._chatYeetDocumentKeydown) {
    if (document.onkeydown === window._chatYeetDocumentKeydown) {
      document.onkeydown = null
    }
    window._chatYeetDocumentKeydown = null
  }

  if (window._chatYeetWindowKeydown) {
    window.removeEventListener("keydown", window._chatYeetWindowKeydown)
    window._chatYeetWindowKeydown = null
  }
