triggerGlobalRefresh(customMessage, reasonMessage) =>
  let countdown = 30;
  const container = document.createElement("div");

  // 1. Add tracking class
  container.classList.add("perchance-sys-notif");

  container.style.cssText = `
    top: -150px;
    left: 50%;
    position: fixed;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    z-index: 99999;
    transition: top 0.5s ease-in-out, opacity 0.5s ease-in-out;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    opacity: 0;
  `;

  const refreshBox = document.createElement("div");
  refreshBox.style.cssText = `
    background: #131516;
    padding: 10px 20px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    font-family: sans-serif;
    font-size: 14px;
    color: white;
    box-shadow: 0px 4px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15), 0px 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    border: 1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    text-align: left;
    gap: 14px;
  `;
  refreshBox.innerHTML = `
    <div style="flex:1; text-align:left; line-height:1.4;">${customMessage}</div>
    <div class="cancelRefreshBtn" style="position:relative; flex-shrink:0; width:36px; height:36px; cursor:pointer;">
      <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 40 40" style="pointer-events:none;">
        <circle cx="20" cy="20" r="12" stroke="#555" stroke-width="2" fill="none"/>
        <circle id="progressCircle" cx="20" cy="20" r="12" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" fill="none"
          stroke-dasharray="75.4" stroke-dashoffset="0" transform="rotate(-90 20 20)"/>
      </svg>
      <i class="fa-solid fa-pause" style="position:absolute; top:50%; left:50%; transform:translate(-50%,-50%); font-size:10px; color:white; pointer-events:none;"></i>
    </div>
  `;
  container.appendChild(refreshBox);

  let reasonBox = null;
  if (reasonMessage) {
    reasonBox = document.createElement("div");
    reasonBox.style.cssText = `
      background: #131516;
      padding: 10px 20px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      font-family: sans-serif;
      font-size: 14px;
      color: white;
      box-shadow: 0px 4px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15), 0px 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
      border: 1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color);
      box-sizing: border-box;
      text-align: left;
      overflow-wrap: break-word;
      white-space:pre-line;
    `;
    reasonBox.innerHTML = reasonMessage;
    container.appendChild(reasonBox);
  }
  
  // 2. DOM Spam Protection (Limit to 4)
  const existingNotifs = document.querySelectorAll('.perchance-sys-notif');
  const maxAllowed = 4;
  if (existingNotifs.length >= maxAllowed) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= existingNotifs.length - maxAllowed; i++) {
      existingNotifs[i].remove();
    }
  }

  document.body.appendChild(container);

  const dismiss = (shouldReload = false) => {
    clearInterval(interval);
    container.style.opacity = "0";
    container.style.top = "-150px";
    setTimeout(() => {
      container.remove();
      if (shouldReload) window.location.reload();
    }, 500);
  };

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (reasonBox) {
      const refreshBoxWidth = refreshBox.offsetWidth;
      reasonBox.style.width = `${refreshBoxWidth}px`;
    }
    container.style.top = "20px";
    container.style.opacity = "1";
  }, 100);

  const circle = container.querySelector("#progressCircle");
  const updateCircle = () => circle.style.strokeDashoffset = (75.4 * (1 - countdown / 30)).toFixed(2);
  updateCircle();

  const interval = setInterval(() => {
    countdown -= 1;
    updateCircle();
    if (countdown <= 0) dismiss(true);
  }, 1000);

  container.querySelector(".cancelRefreshBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
    clearInterval(interval);
    const closeBtnId = "refreshCloseBtn" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 999999);
    refreshBox.innerHTML = `
      <div style="flex:1; text-align:left; line-height:1.4;">
        Refresh cancelled. Turn on ⊝ toggle in 
        <button style="position:relative;display:inline-flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;width:1.4em;height:1.4em;border:none;border-radius:0.35em;background-color:var(--special-color);color:white;cursor:pointer;vertical-align:-0.15em;margin:0 0.15em;padding:0;box-shadow:0px 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size:1em;line-height:1;">chrome_reader_mode</span>
        </button>
        to disable auto-refresh.
      </div>
      <div id="${closeBtnId}" style="flex-shrink:0; width:18px; height:18px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:12px; color:#aaa; cursor:pointer;">✕</div>
    `;
    document.getElementById(closeBtnId).onclick = () => dismiss(false);
    setTimeout(() => dismiss(false), 8000);
  });

triggerHardRefresh(customMessage, reasonMessage) =>
  let countdown = 30
  let interval
  const container = document.createElement("div")

  container.classList.add("perchance-sys-notif")
  container.style.cssText = "top:-150px;left:50%;position:fixed;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:100005;transition:top 0.5s ease-in-out, opacity 0.5s ease-in-out;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:8px;opacity:0;"

  const refreshBox = document.createElement("div")
  refreshBox.style.cssText = "background:#2a0a0a;padding:15px 25px;border-radius:8px;font-family:sans-serif;font-size:15px;font-weight:bold;color:white;box-shadow:0px 4px 15px rgba(255,0,0,0.4);border:2px solid var(--warning-color, #ff4444);display:flex;align-items:center;text-align:left;gap:14px;"

  refreshBox.innerHTML =
    '<div style="flex:1;text-align:left;line-height:1.4;">' + customMessage + '</div>' +
    '<div style="position:relative;flex-shrink:0;width:40px;height:40px;">' +
      '<svg width="40" height="40" viewBox="0 0 40 40">' +
        '<circle cx="20" cy="20" r="14" stroke="#555" stroke-width="3" fill="none"></circle>' +
        '<circle id="hardProgressCircle" cx="20" cy="20" r="14" stroke="#ff4444" stroke-width="3" fill="none" stroke-dasharray="87.96" stroke-dashoffset="0" transform="rotate(-90 20 20)"></circle>' +
        '<text id="hardCountText" x="20" y="21" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle" font-size="14" fill="white">30</text>' +
      '</svg>' +
    '</div>'

  container.appendChild(refreshBox)

  let reasonBox = null
  if (reasonMessage) {
    reasonBox = document.createElement("div")
    reasonBox.style.cssText = "background:#131516;padding:10px 20px;border-radius:8px;font-family:sans-serif;font-size:14px;color:#ffcccc;box-shadow:0px 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);border:1px solid #ff4444;box-sizing:border-box;text-align:left;overflow-wrap:break-word;"
    reasonBox.textContent = reasonMessage
    container.appendChild(reasonBox)
  }
  document.body.appendChild(container)

  const dismiss = () => {
    if (interval) clearInterval(interval)
    container.style.opacity = "0"
    container.style.top = "-150px"
    setTimeout(() => {
      container.remove()
      window.location.reload()
    }, 500)
  }

  setTimeout(() => {
    if (reasonBox) reasonBox.style.width = refreshBox.offsetWidth + "px"
    container.style.top = "10vh"
    container.style.opacity = "1"
  }, 100)

  const circle = container.querySelector("#hardProgressCircle")
  const textEl = container.querySelector("#hardCountText")

  const updateCircle = () => {
    circle.style.strokeDashoffset = (87.96 * (1 - countdown / 30)).toFixed(2)
    textEl.textContent = String(countdown)
  }

  updateCircle()

  interval = setInterval(() => {
    countdown -= 1
    if (countdown < 0) countdown = 0
    updateCircle()
    if (countdown <= 0) dismiss()
  }, 1000)
  
triggerAgeConfirmTimer(customMessage) =>
  let countdown = 60
  const box = document.createElement("div")
  box.style.top = "-80px"
  box.style.left = "50%"
  box.style.position = "fixed"
  box.style.transform = "translateX(-50%)"
  box.style.background = "#131516"
  box.style.padding = "10px 20px"
  box.style.borderRadius = "8px"
  box.style.fontFamily = "sans-serif"
  box.style.fontSize = "14px"
  box.style.color = "white"
  box.style.zIndex = "99999"
  box.style.boxShadow = "0px 4px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15), 0px 1px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)"
  box.style.setProperty("border", "1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color)")
  box.style.display = "flex"
  box.style.alignItems = "center"
  box.style.gap = "12px"
  box.style.transition = "top 0.5s ease-in-out"

  // message
  const msg = document.createElement("div")
  msg.style.flex = "1"
  msg.style.lineHeight = "1.4"
  msg.textContent = customMessage || "Time left for that new user to confirm age."

  // close button
  const closeBtn = document.createElement("span")
  closeBtn.innerText = "✕"
  closeBtn.style.cursor = "pointer"
  closeBtn.style.color = "#aaa"
  closeBtn.style.fontSize = "14px"
  closeBtn.style.marginLeft = "8px"

  // circle timer
  const circleWrap = document.createElement("div")
  circleWrap.style.position = "relative"
  circleWrap.style.flexShrink = "0"
  circleWrap.style.width = "36px"
  circleWrap.style.height = "36px"
  circleWrap.style.pointerEvents = "none"
  circleWrap.style.display = "flex"
  circleWrap.style.alignItems = "center"
  circleWrap.style.justifyContent = "center"
  circleWrap.innerHTML = `
    <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 40 40">
      <circle cx="20" cy="20" r="12" stroke="#555" stroke-width="2" fill="none"></circle>
      <circle id="progressCircle" cx="20" cy="20" r="12" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" fill="none"
        stroke-dasharray="75.4" stroke-dashoffset="0" transform="rotate(-90 20 20)"></circle>
      <text id="countText" x="20" y="21" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="middle"
        font-size="12" fill="white" font-family="sans-serif">60</text>
    </svg>
  `

  box.appendChild(msg)
  box.appendChild(circleWrap)
  box.appendChild(closeBtn)
  document.body.appendChild(box)

  // 1. Declare interval here so dismiss can see it
  let interval;

  // 2. Clear interval automatically whenever dismiss is called
  const dismiss = () => {
    if (interval) clearInterval(interval)
    box.style.top = "-80px"
    setTimeout(() => box.remove(), 500)
  }

  // slide in
  setTimeout(() => { box.style.top = "20px" }, 100)

  // swipe-to-dismiss
  let startX = 0, startY = 0
  box.addEventListener("touchstart", e => {
    startX = e.touches[0].clientX
    startY = e.touches[0].clientY
  })
  box.addEventListener("touchend", e => {
    const dx = Math.abs(startX - e.changedTouches[0].clientX)
    const dy = Math.abs(startY - e.changedTouches[0].clientY)
    if (dx > 50 || dy > 50) {
      dismiss()
    }
  })

  // close button click
  closeBtn.onclick = () => {
    dismiss()
  }

  // countdown logic
  const circle = box.querySelector("#progressCircle")
  const txtEl = box.querySelector("#countText")
  const total = 60
  const circumference = 75.4
  const update = () => {
    const offset = (circumference * (1 - countdown / total)).toFixed(2)
    circle.style.strokeDashoffset = offset
    txtEl.textContent = String(countdown)
  }
  update()

  // 3. Assign the actual loop to our interval variable
  interval = setInterval(() => {
    countdown -= 1
    if (countdown < 0) countdown = 0
    update()
    if (countdown <= 0) {
      dismiss()
    }
  }, 1000)

async showModal(html) =>
  if(!html) html = "<p>?</p>";
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const overlay = Object.assign(document.createElement("div"), { tabIndex: 0 });
    overlay.style.cssText = `inset:0;z-index:9999;position:fixed;display:grid;place-items:center;background-color:rgba(0,0,0,.65);font:16px/1.4 system-ui`;
    overlay.innerHTML = `<div style="position:relative;">
      <button style="position:absolute;z-index:10;top:-12px;right:-12px;width:28px;height:28px;border:none;border-radius:50%;background-color:light-dark(#fff,#444);color:light-dark(#666,#aaa);cursor:pointer;font-size:18px;line-height:1;padding:0;box-shadow:0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.2);">✕</button>
      <div style="text-align:left !important;border-radius:8px;background-color:light-dark(#fff,#222);color:light-dark(#000,#fff);box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.2);">
        ${html}
      </div>
    </div>`;
    
    const [wrapper, closeBtn] = [overlay.firstChild, overlay.querySelector("button")];
    
    // Observer acts as a safety net if the modal gets deleted forcefully
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (!document.body.contains(overlay)) {
        resolve();
        observer.disconnect();
      }
    });
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

    // Normal clean close
    const finish = () => { 
      overlay.remove(); 
      resolve(); 
      observer.disconnect(); 
    };
    
    closeBtn.onclick = finish;
    overlay.onmousedown = e => { if(e.target === overlay) finish(); };
    overlay.onkeydown = e => { if(e.key === "Escape") finish(); };
    
    document.body.append(overlay);
  });

showImageFullscreen(imageUrl) =>
  let overlay = document.createElement('div')
  overlay.style.cssText = `
    inset: 0;
    display: flex;
    position: fixed;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,0.8);
    z-index: 1000000;
    overflow: hidden;
  `
  document.body.appendChild(overlay)

  let img = new Image()
  img.src = imageUrl
  img.style.cssText = `
    max-width: 95%;
    max-height: 95%;
    transition: transform 0.25s ease;
    transform-origin: center center;
    cursor: zoom-in;
  `
  overlay.appendChild(img)

  let zoomed = false

  const toggleZoom = (event) => {
    zoomed = !zoomed

    if (zoomed) {
      const rect = img.getBoundingClientRect()
      let x, y

      if (event.type.startsWith('touch')) {
        const touch = event.changedTouches[0]
        x = touch.clientX - rect.left
        y = touch.clientY - rect.top
      } else {
        x = event.clientX - rect.left
        y = event.clientY - rect.top
      }

      const xPct = (x / rect.width) * 100
      const yPct = (y / rect.height) * 100
      img.style.transformOrigin = `${xPct}% ${yPct}%`
      img.style.transform = 'scale(2.5)'
      img.style.cursor = 'zoom-out'
    } else {
      img.style.transform = 'scale(1)'
      img.style.transformOrigin = 'center center'
      img.style.cursor = 'zoom-in'
    }
  }

  // ✅ Desktop: one click
  if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) {
    img.onclick = toggleZoom
  }

  // ✅ Mobile: double tap
  else {
    let lastTap = 0
    img.ontouchend = e => {
      let now = Date.now()
      if (now - lastTap < 300) toggleZoom(e)
      lastTap = now
    }
  }

  // 🅧 Close on background tap
  overlay.onclick = e => {
    if (e.target === overlay) {
      img.src = ""; // Force Safari to dump the image from RAM
      overlay.remove();
    }
  }

  // 🅧 Close button
  let close = document.createElement('div')
  close.textContent = '✕'
  close.style.cssText = `
    position: absolute;
    top: 12px;
    right: 16px;
    font: 24px/1 sans-serif;
    color: #fff;
    cursor: pointer;
  `
  close.onclick = () => {
    img.src = ""; // Force Safari to dump the image from RAM
    overlay.remove();
  }
  overlay.appendChild(close)

saveToPrivateGallery () =>
  ;(async function () {
    const data = window._currentImageData;
    if (!data) return;

    // ✅ buildConceptBoardSaveInfo already preserves Gallery saveTitle/saveDescription.
    const infoObj = buildConceptBoardSaveInfo(data);

    // ✅ Keep legacy fallback for non-image URLs
    if (data.imageUrl && !/\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|bmp|svg)(\?.*)?$/i.test(data.imageUrl)) {
      const dummyBlob = new Blob([""], { type: "text/plain" });
      await window.t2i_privateGallery.add(dummyBlob, { info: infoObj });
      return;
    }

    function animateImageIntoTarget({imgEl, sourceEl, targetEl, durationMs = 700, wiggle = true}) {
      const srcRect = sourceEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      const tgtRect = targetEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      Object.assign(imgEl.style, {
        top: "25vh",
        position: "fixed",
        left: "25vw",
        width: "50vw",
        height: "50vh",
        margin: "0",
        pointerEvents: "none",
        zIndex: "9999",
        transition: `
          top ${durationMs}ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1),
          left ${durationMs}ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1),
          width ${durationMs}ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1),
          height ${durationMs}ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)
        `,
        objectFit: "contain",
        objectPosition: "center center",
        borderRadius: "3px",
        opacity: "0.7"
      });

      document.body.appendChild(imgEl);
      imgEl.getBoundingClientRect();

      imgEl.style.top = `${tgtRect.top + tgtRect.height / 2}px`;
      imgEl.style.left = `${tgtRect.left + tgtRect.width / 2}px`;
      imgEl.style.width = "0px";
      imgEl.style.height = "0px";

      imgEl.addEventListener("transitionend", () => {
        imgEl.remove();
        if (!wiggle) return;

        targetEl.style.animation = "none";
        void targetEl.offsetWidth; 
        
        targetEl.style.animation = `conceptWiggle 400ms ease-in-out`;
        targetEl.addEventListener("animationend", () => {
          targetEl.style.animation = "";
        }, { once: true });
      }, { once: true });
    }

    const imageUrl = data.imageUrl;

    // --- Route to specific page if requested ---
    let addOpts = { info: infoObj };
    let destType = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveDestType');
    let destName = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveDestName');
    if (destType === 'specific' && destName && destName.trim() !== '') {
      addOpts.folder = destName.trim();
    }

    // Gallery saves can stay link-only or keep a local image copy.
    // In both modes, infoObj keeps the original Gallery image URL as source metadata.
    const storageMode = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveStorageMode') || 'link';
    let imageBlob = null;

    if (storageMode === 'file') {
      const response = await fetch(imageUrl);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Could not save Gallery image copy (${response.status}).`);
      imageBlob = await response.blob();
    }

    await window.t2i_privateGallery.add(imageBlob, addOpts);

    let imgEl = document.createElement("img");
    imgEl.src = imageUrl;

    let sourceEl = document.querySelector(`img[src="${imageUrl}"]`);
    if (!sourceEl) sourceEl = document.body;

    let targetEl = document.getElementById("floatingBtn");

    animateImageIntoTarget({
      imgEl,
      sourceEl,
      targetEl,
      durationMs: 700,
      wiggle: true
    });
  })()

silentSaveToConceptBoard(url, senderInfo) =>
  ;(async () => {
    if (!url) return;

    // --- Route to specific page if requested ---
    let addOpts = { info: senderInfo };
    let destType = localStorage.getItem('autoSaveDestType');
    let destName = localStorage.getItem('autoSaveDestName');
    if (destType === 'specific' && destName && destName.trim() !== '') {
      addOpts.folder = destName.trim();
    }

    // Handle non-image URLs
    if (!/\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|bmp|svg)(\?.*)?$/i.test(url)) {
      const dummyBlob = new Blob([""], { type: "text/plain" });
      await window.t2i_privateGallery.add(dummyBlob, addOpts);
      return;
    }
    
    // ✅ Auto-saved hosted image links are now link-only.
    try {
      await window.t2i_privateGallery.add(null, addOpts);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Failed to auto-save image link to Concept Board:", e);
    }
  })() 
  
downloadImage (url) =>
  ;(async () => {
    const res = await fetch(url);
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const extMatch = url.match(/\.(jpeg|jpg|png|webp)(\?.*)?$/i);
    const ext = extMatch ? extMatch[1] : "image";
    a.download = "image." + ext;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
  })()
